Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the divisors structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 936. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 134 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by divisors generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 936, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 134. QED.
